- **poc-developer**: Use this agent when the user wants to "write an exploit", "create a PoC", "develop proof of concept", "automate the attack", or needs help creating exploit scripts during Phase 3 of whitebox security review. ([allsmog/vuln-scout](https://github.com/allsmog/vuln-scout/blob/HEAD/vuln-scout/agents/poc-developer.md) · ★24)
- **poc-engineer**: Given a confirmed finding + its context worksheet, produces the smallest self-contained crate that reproduces the flaw — feature-gated vulnerable/fixed arms, asserting the exploit succeeds on the vulnerable arm and is rejected on the fixed arm. Emits the [PoC-*] evidence tier; downgrades to [PoC-ATTEMPTED] + prose on any blocker, never fabricates a passing test. ([solanabr/auditor-skill](https://github.com/solanabr/auditor-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/poc-engineer.md) · ★54)
- **poc-evaluation-lead**: Use to design and run a proof-of-concept or pilot — decide IF a POC is warranted, write measurable success + exit criteria a champion signs, scope a time-boxed plan, and score it into a technical win. NOT for the live demo (sales-engineer) or the security questionnaire. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/sales-engineering/agents/poc-evaluation-lead.md) · ★7)
- **poc-generator**: Generate Foundry proof-of-concept tests that validate HIGH and CRITICAL security findings. Produces runnable Solidity test files with attack contracts, setup scaffolding, and pass/fail assertions. Use after running entrypoint-analysis or call-analysis when findings need validation. ([asyncswap/skills](https://github.com/asyncswap/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/solidity-language-server/agents/poc-generator.md) · ★1)

- **poc-validator**: Delegates to this agent when the user wants to validate a vulnerability finding with a safe Proof of Concept, eliminate false positives from scan results, automatically generate and execute PoC scripts for confirmed vulnerabilities, or verify that a reported bug is real before including it in a pentest report. - **pocketbase-docs-researcher**: Use this agent proactively when you need comprehensive research on PocketBase topics.
